Use when asked to clean up, polish, prepare, typeset or get a scanned score ready to share, print or send to a choir, after omr-halbestunde or omr-musescore-convert, or when a score imported from… ([wattdave/ai-score-editing-workflows](https://github.com/wattdave/ai-score-editing-workflows/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/score-polish-for-sharing))
- **score-pr**: Score how dangerous a pull request or branch is, 0-100, from its blast radius and one-way or two-way door, and name its review tier. Use when the user asks how risky a change is, "is this a one-way door", "what's the blast radius", or how much review it deserves. ([denisulmer/skills](https://github.com/denisulmer/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/score-pr))
